| `SV-0039` | `systemverilog` / `sv_2017`+`sv_2023` (`verilog_2005` correctly rejects — SVA is SystemVerilog-only, absent from IEEE 1364-2005 Annex A) | `1.0.0`–`1.0.168` (the two SVA sequence-implication operators `\|->` overlapped and `\|=>` non-overlapped were NEVER in the grammar — `git log -S '\|->'` on `grammars/systemverilog.ebnf` is empty; the LRM-markdown→EBNF extractor split the `\|`-prefixed operators on `\|`, the EBNF alternation metachar, leaving the mangled `implies` (`->`) / `or_assign` (`\|=`) remnants in `prop_primary`) | `1.0.168` | `(internal — surfaced 2026-07-23 by the SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.2 rejects-valid family refresh: SVA implication/property is the #1 construct family at 101 rows; tool-pinned four ways — a minimal repro, a token scan, scoped --trace-rules, and IEEE 1800-2017 grounding — the external corpus as a bug-finding oracle)` | `2026-07-23` | `Released` | `n/a (internal; no external downstream report)` | Repro (against `1.0.0`–`1.0.168`): `printf 'module top; reg clk; reg a,b;\na1: assert property (@(posedge clk) a \|-> b);\nendmodule\n' \| ./rust/target/debug/parseability_probe --parse systemverilog /dev/stdin --profile sv_2017` → "Parser did not consume full input" (stuck at `\|->`); the implication-free `assert property (@(posedge clk) p3)` parses exit-0 (the gap is the operator, not the RHS). Token scan: the only `>`/`\|` operator tokens were `implies` (`->`), `or_assign` (`\|=`), `sequence_implies` (`=>`), `nonblocking_implies` (`->>`) — no `\|->`, no `\|=>`. IEEE 1800-2017 A.2.10: `property_expr ::= … \| sequence_expr \|-> property_expr \| sequence_expr \|=> property_expr` (used pervasively §17/§23; `vpiOverlapImplyOp`/`vpiNonOverlapImplyOp` §83). | The two core SVA sequence-implication operators were entirely absent — no `overlapped_implication`/`non_overlapped_implication` token and no `prop_primary` branch consumed them, so every `sequence \|-> property` / `sequence \|=> property` assertion rejected at the operator. Root cause of the omission: the LRM-markdown→EBNF extractor split the `\|`-prefixed operators on `\|` (the alternation metachar); the LRM-*extracted* grammar lacks them too. Orthogonal to H.12.5.8 (whose 12/12 matrix tested only the word operators until/or/and/intersect/within/##). | **Fixed (`SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.3`, `PGEN-SV-CORPUS-GRAD-0018`, 2026-07-23, grammar-only):** added the two tokens `overlapped_implication := "\|->"` / `non_overlapped_implication := "\|=>"` (profile-gated `["sv_2017","sv_2023"]` like `nonblocking_implies`/`SV-0023` — SV-only, so `verilog_2005` is byte-inert) and mirrored the two A.2.10 branches `sequence_expr <op> property_expr` into `prop_primary_sv_2017` and `prop_primary_sv_2023` (census `1466`→`1468`, +2 token rules). VERIFIED (all seeds 0/7/42): repro matrix `a \|-> b`/`a \|=> b`/`a \|-> p3` (named prop)/`a \|=> ##1 b` REJECT→ACCEPT under `sv_2017`/`sv_2023`, `verilog_2005` correctly REJECTS; **full external corpus 16,336: pass 9,361→9,433 (+72, timeout 9→9 unchanged), ZERO per-suite regressions** (ispras +34, verilator +21, Surelog +6, opentitan +4, sv-tests +4, iverilog +3 — all SVA-assertion files); adjudication baseline rejects-valid 543→481, accepts-invalid 21 UNCHANGED (identical set — no over-acceptance), unexplained 564→502; `sv_cert_recognized_union_gate` GREEN on an evidence-grounded re-baseline — the 2 new tokens are POSITIVELY WITNESSED (total 1343→1345, canonical witness 1326→1328, union witness 1337→1339, proof 6 / UNKNOWN 11/0 / residual `[]` unchanged, still `fully_certified_via_union`); `verilog_2005_conformance_gate` GREEN byte-inert (cert `1115/328/773/14`, corpus matrix 240/0, profile-orphans 0 — the gating keeps v2005 untouched); `sv_stimuli_quality_gate` PASS (`closed_loop_replay_targets_total` 120→**126**: the 4 new `prop_primary` branches + 2 token rules are new closed-loop generation targets, feeding `SV-REPLAY-DEBT`); `ast_shape_contract_gate` 18/0; `sv_external_corpus_triage_gate` 14/14; clippy source-strict + dual-feature lib tests green. | `1.0.169` | **LANGUAGE change, fixes a real parse bug, schema `16`→`17`:** the overlapped `\|->` and non-overlapped `\|=>` sequence-implication operators (IEEE 1800-2017 A.2.10) now parse under `sv_2017`/`sv_2023`, emitting `{kind:"overlapped_implication", lhs, rhs}` / `{kind:"non_overlapped_implication", lhs, rhs}` in `prop_primary`. Strictly more permissive (only previously-REJECTED assertions gain parses; `verilog_2005` still rejects — SVA is SV-only). Schema bumps `16`→`17` because two new `prop_primary` branches carrying new return annotations are added (a user-visible shape change — a consumer exhaustively matching `prop_primary` kinds now sees two new values). Surfaced corpus-first by the graduation campaign — the SECOND defect (after `SV-0038`) the SV-CORPUS-GRAD adjudication pipeline caught and closed end-to-end; the #1 rejects-valid family. |
| `SV-0040` | `systemverilog` / `sv_2017`+`sv_2023` (modport is SystemVerilog-only; `verilog_2005` correctly rejects modport) | `1.0.0`–`1.0.169` (the LRM A.2.9 `{ , modport_simple_port }` repetition was never in `modport_simple_ports_declaration`, and the SV port_direction keyword `ref` was never in `reserved_non_keyword_identifier_sv`) | `1.0.169` | `(internal — surfaced 2026-07-23 by SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.4: interface/modport is the #3 rejects-valid family at 50 rows; the external corpus as a bug-finding oracle)` | `2026-07-23` | `Released` | `n/a (internal; no external downstream report)` | Repro (against `1.0.0`–`1.0.169`): `printf 'interface I;\n  logic a,b,c,d;\n  modport master(input a, b, output c, d);\nendinterface\n' \| ./rust/target/debug/parseability_probe --parse systemverilog /dev/stdin --profile sv_2017` → "Parser did not consume full input" (`furthest_position=58`, at the shared-direction port `b`); the single-port control `modport master(input a, output c)` parses exit-0. Coupled `ref` repro (the mid-measurement regression): `modport phy(input addr, ref data)` — a REF-direction modport — REJECTED after the fix's part 1 alone, because the new greedy port list consumed `ref` as a port name. IEEE 1800-2017 §25/A.2.9: `modport_simple_ports_declaration ::= port_direction modport_simple_port { , modport_simple_port }` (verified verbatim `docs/systemverilog/2017/md/section-25-interfaces.md:139`); IEEE 1800 Table B.1 reserves `ref`. | Two coupled root causes. (1) `modport_simple_ports_declaration` (`grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:526`) was `port_direction modport_simple_port` — MISSING the LRM `{ , modport_simple_port }` repetition, so one `port_direction` admitted only ONE port and the outer `modport_item ( comma modport_ports_declaration )*` could not open a new group at a bare shared-direction port (`b` is not a `port_direction`/attribute/import-export/clocking), so `rparen` was expected and `,` found. (2) `ref` — a genuine SV `port_direction` (`port_direction_sv_only := kw_ref`) and an IEEE 1800 Table B.1 reserved keyword — was erroneously ABSENT from `reserved_non_keyword_identifier_sv` (which already lists `input`/`output`/`inout`), so `ref` wrongly matched `port_identifier`; it fell through the `SV-0036` net, which appended only the `verilog_2005`-reserved delta to the SV list and `ref` is SV-*only*-reserved. Part 2 is REQUIRED for part 1: the engine's `*` does not backtrack across the rule boundary ([[project_earlier_always_matches_unsound_backtracking]] class), so the shared-direction repetition is only sound once `ref` fails `port_identifier` (as `output` already did). | **Fixed (`SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.4`, `PGEN-SV-CORPUS-GRAD-0019`, 2026-07-23, grammar-only, two coupled parts):** (1) `modport_simple_ports_declaration := port_direction modport_simple_port ( comma modport_simple_port )* -> {direction: $1, ports: [$2, $3::2*]}` (the `[$first, $rep::2*]` extraction-spread; emitted shape `port` → `ports` array); (2) added `ref` to `reserved_non_keyword_identifier_sv` (`:402`); `verilog_2005` keeps its own list (`_v2005:405`, no `ref`) so it stays byte-inert. No new rule/token (census unchanged 1468). VERIFIED: repro matrix (control / shared-direction / modport-expr / `ref`-direction / the 2 formerly-regressed verilator virtual-interface files) all ACCEPT under `sv_2017`; `verilog_2005` correctly REJECTS modport; AST carries `{direction:{kind:"input"}, ports:[{kind:"identifier",name:{body:"a"}},{...b}]}`. **Full external corpus 16,336: pass 9,433 → 9,459 (+26 net raw)** — 29 gains (verilator 13 / ispras-sv-tests 11 / Surelog 2 / sv2v 2 / opentitan 1) − 3 raw pass→fail (three `deferred:v2005_profile_lane` ivtest `.v` files declaring `reg ref;`: under `sv_2017` `ref` is now correctly reserved so they reject there — they stay v2005-lane-deferred and still ACCEPT under `verilog_2005`; a per-FILE pass-set `comm` diff, not the net-per-suite count, proved this — the net check had masked the 2 mid-measurement regressions the `ref` part later recovered). **Adjudication baseline: rejects-valid 481 → 454 (−27** verilator −13 / ispras-sv-tests −11 / sv2v −2 / Surelog −1**), accepts-invalid 21 → 21 (IDENTICAL set — no over-acceptance), 0 new rejects-valid** (`comm`-verified), unexplained 502 → 475. **NO REGRESSION proof:** `verilog_2005_conformance_gate` GREEN byte-inert (lint orphans 0, corpus 240/0, cert deterministic seeds 0/7/42; `adjudication_manifest_v2005.tsv` BYTE-IDENTICAL, `results_v2005.tsv` sorted-identical); `systemverilog_parser_book_gate` GREEN; `sv_stimuli_quality_gate` PASS (`closed_loop_replay_targets_total` 126 UNCHANGED — the modport `*` repetition adds no new closed-loop generation target); `sv_cert_recognized_union_gate` GREEN (BYTE-INERT vs the existing pinned contract — canonical UNKNOWN=11, union UNKNOWN=0, union witness 1339, residual `[]`, deterministic seeds 0/7/42, still `fully_certified_via_union`; NO re-baseline needed because no rule/token was added). | `1.0.170` | **LANGUAGE + SHAPE change, schema `17`→`18`:** comma-shared-direction modport port lists (`modport m(input a, b, output c, d)`, incl. `ref` direction groups) now parse under `sv_2017`/`sv_2023`, and `modport_simple_ports_declaration` emits `{direction, ports:[…]}` (a `ports` ARRAY) where it emitted a single `port` field. Strictly more permissive for grammar-valid modports; `verilog_2005` still rejects modport (SV-only). The coupled `ref` reservation additionally makes `ref` correctly non-usable as an identifier under the SV profiles (`verilog_2005` unaffected — same SV-vs-v2005 reserved-keyword split as `SV-0036`). A consumer reading `modport_simple_ports_declaration` must adopt the `ports` array shape. Surfaced corpus-first by the graduation campaign — the third defect (after `SV-0038`/`SV-0039`) the SV-CORPUS-GRAD pipeline caught and closed end-to-end. |
| `SV-0041` | `systemverilog` / ALL profiles (`sv_2017`+`sv_2023`+`verilog_2005` — the based-literal white-space rule is identical in IEEE 1800-2017 §5.7.1 and IEEE 1364-2005 §3.5.1) | `1.0.0`–`1.0.170` (the `integral_number` terminal `grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:439` never admitted the LRM-legal white space between the size and the `'` or between the base format and the value) | `1.0.170` | `(internal — surfaced 2026-07-23 by SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.5: number-literal spaced-based is the #6 rejects-valid family at 22 rows — the chapter-5 lexical cluster repeatedly flagged since .2; the external corpus as a bug-finding oracle)` | `2026-07-23` | `Released` | `n/a (internal; no external downstream report)` | Repro (against `1.0.0`–`1.0.170`): `echo "module m; parameter logic [31:0] A = 32'h 0000_0001; endmodule" \| ./rust/target/debug/parseability_probe --parse systemverilog /dev/stdin --profile sv_2017` → "Parser did not consume full input" (`furthest_position=41`, at the base↔value space); the contiguous control `32'h0000_0001` parses exit-0; also `32 'h 12ab_f001` (size↔`'` space, the §5.7.1 example) and a `verilog_2005` `5'h 0` reject at HEAD. IEEE 1800-2017 §5.7.1 / IEEE 1364-2005 §3.5.1 (verbatim, `docs/systemverilog/2017/md/section-1024-…-error-shall-be-reported.md:207-209`): "The apostrophe character and the base format character shall not be separated by any white space. … The unsigned number token shall immediately follow the base format, optionally preceded by white space." | `integral_number` (`grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:439`) is a SINGLE regex terminal `([0-9][0-9_]*)?'[sS]?[dDhHoObB][0-9a-fA-FxXzZ?_]+`; the generated layout skipper consumes white space BEFORE a terminal but NOT inside one match, so the terminal could not span the two seams the LRM permits (size↔`'`, base↔value). Because `integral_number` is not profile-split, the gap hit ALL profiles. | **Fixed (`SV-CORPUS-GRAD.3.5`, `PGEN-SV-CORPUS-GRAD-0020`, 2026-07-23, grammar-only, one terminal):** `integral_number := ([0-9][0-9_]*[ \t]*)?'[sS]?[dDhHoObB][ \t]*[0-9a-fA-FxXzZ?_]+` — added horizontal-white-space `[ \t]*` at exactly the two LRM-legal seams; the `'[sS]?[dDhHoObB]` base specifier stays contiguous (LRM forbids interior white space) and the value charset is UNCHANGED, so the LRM-illegal `'d -6` (minus between base and value) and interior white space inside the value still reject. No new rule/token (census 1345 unchanged); `body:$1` shape unchanged (spaced literals were 100% unparseable ⇒ additive, no lock broken). VERIFIED: all 22 family rows + `32 'h …` + a v2005 `5'h 0` REJECT→ACCEPT, `'d -6` still REJECTS; **main sv_2017 external corpus 16,336: pass 9,459→9,585 (+126** opentitan 83 / iverilog 14 / sv-tests 13 / ispras 8 / Surelog 6 / verilator 2**), timeout 10→9; adjudication rejects-valid 454→432 (−22 = the whole #6 family, `comm`-verified exactly the 22 keyed rows, ZERO new), accepts-invalid 21→21 (BYTE-IDENTICAL set)**; **CROSS-PROFILE bonus — `verilog_2005` lane 2,459: pass 2,107→2,126 (+19), rejects-valid 135→116 (−19, 0 new), accepts-invalid 14→14 (identical)**. **NO REGRESSION: 0 pass→fail per-FILE in BOTH lanes** (strictly additive); `sv_cert_recognized_union_gate` GREEN byte-inert (no rule/token ⇒ census 1345, canonical UNKNOWN=11, union residual `[]`, `fully_certified_via_union`); `verilog_2005_conformance_gate` GREEN (curated matrix 240/0 has no spaced-literal case; cert `1115/328/773/14`); `sv_stimuli_quality_gate` PASS (`closed_loop_replay_targets_total` 126 → 125, −1: one `sv_2017` closed-loop replay target newly witnessed — a replay-debt gap CLOSED feeding `SV-REPLAY-DEBT`; a decrease is an improvement and no target can regress under a strictly-more-permissive change); `ast_shape_contract_gate` 18/0; `sv_external_corpus_triage_gate` 14/14. | `1.0.171` | **LANGUAGE change, fixes a real parse bug, schema `18` UNCHANGED:** based integer literals with LRM-legal white space between the size and the `'` (`32 'h5`) and between the base format and the value (`32'h 5`) now parse under ALL profiles (`sv_2017`/`sv_2023`/`verilog_2005` — IEEE 1800-2017 §5.7.1 and IEEE 1364-2005 §3.5.1 share the rule). Strictly more permissive (only previously-REJECTED literals gain parses; `'` still cannot be separated from its base format, and a `-`/white space cannot appear inside the value). NO shape change — `integral_number` is a terminal whose `body` is the whole matched string (now optionally including the interior spaces), so schema stays `18`. The FIRST cross-profile SV-CORPUS-GRAD fix and the FOURTH defect (after `SV-0038`/`SV-0039`/`SV-0040`) the graduation pipeline caught and closed end-to-end; the #6 rejects-valid family. |
